p\in{\llbracket\varphi_{q}\rrbracket}
and not
q\in{\llbracket\varphi_{q}\rrbracket}
.

All in all, every positive clause holds for
p
and none of the formulas in the negative clauses holds for
p
.
Therefore,
p\in{\llbracket{\bigwedge_{q\in Q^{\prime}}}\psi_{q}\rrbracket}
.
For every
q\in Q\setminus Q_{*}
,
\psi_{q}
is either a positive clause that does not hold for
q
, or a negative clause negating a formula that holds for
q
.
If there are
q\in Q_{*}
,
\psi_{*}
is false for all of them.
Consequently, no
q\in{\llbracket{\bigwedge_{q\in Q^{\prime}}}\psi_{q}\rrbracket}
.

Proof of
(Lemma
5
, distinction completeness)

If
\varphi
distinguishes
p
from every
q\in Q
, then
\mathsf{expr}(\varphi)\in\mathsf{Win}_{\operatorname{a}}({{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}(}p,Q{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5})}}_{\mathtt{a}}^{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}\smash{\scriptscriptstyle}})
.

Proof

By induction on the structure of
\varphi
with arbitrary
p,Q
.

•

Case
\langle b\rangle\varphi
.

So
p\in{\llbracket\langle b\rangle\varphi\rrbracket}

and
Q\cap{\llbracket\langle b\rangle\varphi\rrbracket}=\varnothing
.
By the semantics of HML, there are
p^{\prime},Q^{\prime}
with
p\mathrel{\smash{\xrightarrow{b}}}p^{\prime}

and
Q\mathrel{\smash{\xrightarrow{b}}}Q^{\prime}

such that
p^{\prime}\in{\llbracket\varphi\rrbracket}
and
Q^{\prime}\cap{\llbracket\varphi\rrbracket}=\varnothing
.
By induction hypothesis,
\mathsf{expr}(\varphi)\in\mathsf{Win}_{\operatorname{a}}({{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}(}p^{\prime},Q^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5})}}_{\mathtt{a}}^{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}\smash{\scriptscriptstyle}})
.
Thus, as the game must contain
{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}(}p,Q{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5})}}_{\mathtt{a}}^{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}\smash{\scriptscriptstyle}}\mathrel{\smash{\mathrel{\hbox to55.49pt{\vbox to14.76pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 24.74287pt\lower-4.88101pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}{{}}{}
{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}}{}{}{}{{{}{}}}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{}{}{{{}{}}}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{}{}{{}}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 2.93 0 L 7.71 0}{fill:none} {{}{{}}{}{}{{}}{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{-1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{-1.0}{2.33098pt}{0.0pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-dasharray,stroke-dashoffset} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-linecap} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-linejoin} \lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 2.93 3.4 C 2.39 1.36 1.2 0.4 0 0 C 1.2 -0.4 2.39 -1.36 2.93 -3.4}{fill:none} \lxSVG@closescope \lxSVG@closescope }}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}}}{{}{{}}{}{}{{}}{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{5.78502pt}{0.0pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-dasharray,stroke-dashoffset} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-linecap} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-linejoin} \lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -2.93 3.4 C -2.39 1.36 -1.2 0.4 0 0 C -1.2 -0.4 -2.39 -1.36 -2.93 -3.4}{fill:none} \lxSVG@closescope \lxSVG@closescope }}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{}{{
{}{}}}{
{}{}}
{{}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}}{{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{}{{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{3.0pt}{-1.548pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{69}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{}{{
{}{}}}{
{}{}}
{{}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}}{{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{}{{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-21.40987pt}{2.798pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{69}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}(}p^{\prime},Q^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5})}}_{\mathtt{a}}^{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}\smash{\scriptscriptstyle}}
,
and using the definition of formula prices,

\mathsf{upd}^{-1}(\mathsf{expr}(\varphi),(-1,0,0,0,0,0))=\mathsf{expr}(\varphi)-(-1,0,0,0,0,0)=\mathsf{expr}(\langle b\rangle\varphi)\in\mathsf{Win}_{\operatorname{a}}({{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}(}p,Q{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5})}}_{\mathtt{a}}^{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}\smash{\scriptscriptstyle}}))
.

•

Case
{\bigwedge}\Psi
.

So
p\in{\llbracket{\bigwedge}\Psi\rrbracket}

and
Q\cap{\llbracket{\bigwedge}\Psi\rrbracket}=\varnothing
.
Consider all
\Psi^{\prime}\subseteq\Psi
that cover
Q
such that there is a
\psi_{q}\in\Psi^{\prime}
precisely for every
q\in Q
and

Q\cap{\llbracket{\bigwedge}\Psi^{\prime}\rrbracket}=Q\cap{\llbracket{\bigwedge_{q\in Q}}\psi_{q}\rrbracket}=\varnothing
.
By the semantics of HML,
p\in{\llbracket\varphi_{q}\rrbracket}
for every positive
\psi_{q}=\varphi_{q}
, and
p\notin{\llbracket\bar{\varphi}_{q}\rrbracket}
for every negative
\psi_{q}=\neg\bar{\varphi}_{q}
.
Also, for every
q
, there must either be a positive
\psi_{q}=\varphi_{q}
such that
q\notin{\llbracket\varphi_{q}\rrbracket}
or a negative
\psi_{q}=\neg\bar{\varphi}_{q}
such that
q\in{\llbracket\bar{\varphi}_{q}\rrbracket}
.
So, for every
q
, either
\varphi_{q}
distinguishes
p
from
q
or
\bar{\varphi}_{q}
distinguishes
q
from
p
.
If there are any positive
\psi_{q}
, consider any way of selecting a highest
\psi_{q}
, denote it by
\psi_{*}
and denote
Q_{*}=Q\cap{\llbracket\psi_{*}\rrbracket}
as well as
Q^{\prime}=Q\setminus Q_{*}
.
With the induction hypothesis,

\mathsf{expr}(\varphi_{*})=e_{*}\in\mathsf{Win}_{\operatorname{a}}({{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}(}p,Q_{*}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5})}}_{\mathtt{a}}^{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}\smash{\scriptscriptstyle}})

and, for
q\in Q^{\prime}
,
each
\mathsf{expr}(\varphi_{q})=e_{q}\in\mathsf{Win}_{\operatorname{a}}({{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}(}p,\{q\}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5})}}_{\mathtt{a}}^{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}\smash{\scriptscriptstyle}})
and
\mathsf{expr}(\bar{\varphi}_{q})=\bar{e}_{q}\in\mathsf{Win}_{\operatorname{a}}({{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}(}q,\{p\}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5})}}_{\mathtt{a}}^{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}\smash{\scriptscriptstyle}})
.
So if there are
\varphi_{q}
from positive clauses, then
\mathsf{upd}^{-1}(e_{q},(\mathtt{min}_{\{\!1,4\!\}},0,0,0,0,0))=\sup(e_{q},((e_{q})_{1},0,0,(e_{q})_{1},0,0))\in\mathsf{Win}_{\operatorname{a}}({{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}(}p,q{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5})}}_{\mathtt{a}}^{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\land}})
,
and, for negative clauses,
\mathsf{upd}^{-1}(\bar{e}_{q},(\mathtt{min}_{\{\!1,5\!\}},0,0,0,0,-1))
evaluates to the
\sup(\bar{e}_{q}-(0,0,0,0,0,-1),\allowbreak((\bar{e}_{q})_{1},0,0,0,(\bar{e}_{q})_{1},0))\in\mathsf{Win}_{\operatorname{a}}({{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}(}p,q{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5})}}_{\mathtt{a}}^{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\land}})
.
Both kinds of levels are reached through
(0,0,0,\mathtt{min}_{\{\!3,4\!\}},0,0)
updates.
The attacker hence wins at the defender position
{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}(}p,Q^{\prime},Q_{*}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5})}}_{\mathtt{d}}^{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}\smash{\scriptscriptstyle}}
regardless of the defender decisions if the energy level is at

\displaystyle e_{\operatorname{a}}\mathrel{\coloneqq}\sup(

\displaystyle\;\{e_{q},((e_{q})_{1},0,(e_{q})_{1},(e_{q})_{1},0,0)

\displaystyle\qquad\mid q\in Q^{\prime}\land\psi_{q}=\varphi_{q}\land e_{q}=\mathsf{expr}(\varphi_{q})\}

\displaystyle\cup

\displaystyle\;\{\bar{e}_{q}-(0,0,0,0,0,-1),((\bar{e}_{q})_{1},0,(\bar{e}_{q})_{4},(\bar{e}_{q})_{4},(\bar{e}_{q})_{1},0)

\displaystyle\qquad\mid q\in Q^{\prime}\land\psi_{q}=\neg\bar{\varphi}_{q}\land\bar{e}_{q}=\mathsf{expr}(\bar{\varphi}_{q})\}

\displaystyle\cup

\displaystyle\;\{e_{*},((e_{*})_{1},0,(e_{*})_{1},0,0,0)

\displaystyle\qquad\mid Q_{*}\neq\varnothing\}\;)

or above.

\mathsf{upd}^{-1}(e_{\operatorname{a}},(0,-1,0,0,0,0))=e_{\operatorname{a}}+(0,1,0,0,0,0)\in\mathsf{Win}_{\operatorname{a}}({{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}(}p,Q{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5})}}_{\mathtt{a}}^{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}\smash{\scriptscriptstyle}})
by the game structure.

e_{\operatorname{a}}+(1,0,0,0,0,0)
equals
\mathsf{expr}({\bigwedge_{q\in Q^{\prime}\cup\{*\}}}\psi_{q})
, which must be below or equal
\mathsf{expr}({\bigwedge}\Psi)
as the definition is in terms of suprema and
\Psi^{\prime}\subseteq\Psi
.
With the upward closure of winning budgets,
\mathsf{expr}({\bigwedge}\Psi)\in\mathsf{Win}_{\operatorname{a}}({{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}(}p,Q{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5})}}_{\mathtt{a}}^{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}\smash{\scriptscriptstyle}})
.

Proof of
(Theorem
3.2
, correctness of cleverness)

Assume
e_{4}\in\{0,1,\infty\}
,
e_{4}\leq e_{3}
, and that
e_{5}>1
implies
e_{3}=e_{4}
. Then, the attacker wins
\mathcal{G}_{\blacktriangle}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}(}p_{0},Q_{0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5})}}_{\mathtt{a}}^{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}\smash{\scriptscriptstyle}},e]
precisely if they win
\mathcal{G}_{\triangle}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}(}p_{0},Q_{0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5})}}_{\mathtt{a}}^{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}\smash{\scriptscriptstyle}},e]
.

\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}}}\rvert\cdot\lvert\mathord{G}\rvert^{N}\cdot(o+\lvert\mathord{G}\rvert^{(N-1)\cdot o}))
time, using
\mathcal{O}(\lvert\mathord{G}\rvert^{N})
space for the output.

Proof

A position is only updated if a successor has been updated in a way leading to the discovery of winning budgets below those that already were known.
Each energy can trigger such an update only once at each position.
So, the first tentative set of winning budgets assigned bounds the further updates in the order of energies below it.
These are are polynomially bounded by the size of the
N
-dimensional hypercube / grid containing these first tentative budgets and the zero-vector.
Each first assignment is bounded in each dimension by the length of simple paths originating from a position, which can be over-approximated by
\lvert\mathord{G}\rvert
for each position.

Two things follow from this:
First, the amount of antichains in the grid of occurring tentative budgets is bounded by
\lvert\mathord{G}\rvert^{N-1}
,
leading to a space complexity due to
\mathsf{attacker\_win}
of
\lvert\mathord{G}\rvert\cdot\lvert\mathord{G}\rvert^{N-1}
.
Second, the points in the grid bound the proper updates a position can experience to
\lvert\mathord{G}\rvert^{N}
.
Collectively, these can trigger at most move-many updates, so
\lvert\mathord{\mathrel{\smash{\mathrel{\hbox to10.35pt{\vbox to9.76pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.17331pt\lower-4.88101pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}{{}}{}
{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}}{}{}{}{{{}{}}}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{}{}{{{}{}}}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{}{}{{}}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 2.93 0 L 7.71 0}{fill:none} {{}{{}}{}{}{{}}{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{-1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{-1.0}{2.33098pt}{0.0pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-dasharray,stroke-dashoffset} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-linecap} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-linejoin} \lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 2.93 3.4 C 2.39 1.36 1.2 0.4 0 0 C 1.2 -0.4 2.39 -1.36 2.93 -3.4}{fill:none} \lxSVG@closescope \lxSVG@closescope }}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}}}{{}{{}}{}{}{{}}{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{5.78502pt}{0.0pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-dasharray,stroke-dashoffset} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-linecap} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-linejoin} \lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -2.93 3.4 C -2.39 1.36 -1.2 0.4 0 0 C -1.2 -0.4 -2.39 -1.36 -2.93 -3.4}{fill:none} \lxSVG@closescope \lxSVG@closescope }}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{}{{

{{}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}}{{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{}{{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{3.0pt}{-1.548pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{69}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{}{{

}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{1.1597pt}{1.548pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{69}\lxSVG@closescope }}}

\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}}}\rvert\cdot\lvert\mathord{G}\rvert^{N}
bounds the updates.
Every update must consider up to out-degree
o
many successors.
At defender nodes, the update may take
\lvert\mathord{G}\rvert^{(N-1)\cdot o}
combinations into account.
This culminates in a time complexity of
\mathcal{O}(\lvert\mathord{\mathrel{\smash{\mathrel{\hbox to10.35pt{\vbox to9.76pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.17331pt\lower-4.88101pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}{{}}{}

.

Proof of
(Lemma
7
, full spectroscopy complexity)

Time complexity of computing winning budgets for the full spectroscopy energy game
\mathcal{G}_{\triangle}
is in
2^{\mathcal{O}(\lvert\mathord{\mathcal{P}}\rvert\cdot 2^{\lvert\mathord{\mathcal{P}}\rvert})}

in

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle+\lvert\mathord{\mathrel{\smash{\xrightarrow{\cdot}}}}\rvert\cdot 2^{\lvert\mathord{\mathcal{P}}\rvert}\cdot\lvert\mathord{\mathcal{P}}\rvert^{6+C_{2}\cdot 2^{\lvert\mathord{\mathcal{P}}\rvert}}\cdot 3^{6\cdot\lvert\mathord{\mathcal{P}}\rvert+\lvert\mathord{\mathcal{P}}\rvert\cdot C_{2}\cdot 2^{\lvert\mathord{\mathcal{P}}\rvert}}+\lvert\mathord{\mathcal{P}}\rvert^{8+C_{2}\cdot 2^{\lvert\mathord{\mathcal{P}}\rvert}}\cdot 3^{7\cdot\lvert\mathord{\mathcal{P}}\rvert+\lvert\mathord{\mathcal{P}}\rvert\cdot C_{2}\cdot 2^{\lvert\mathord{\mathcal{P}}\rvert}})

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle+\lvert\mathord{\mathrel{\smash{\xrightarrow{\cdot}}}}\rvert\cdot\lvert\mathord{\mathcal{P}}\rvert^{C_{5}\cdot 2^{\lvert\mathord{\mathcal{P}}\rvert}}\cdot 2^{C_{6}\lvert\mathord{\mathcal{P}}\rvert\cdot 2^{\lvert\mathord{\mathcal{P}}\rvert}}+\lvert\mathord{\mathcal{P}}\rvert^{C_{7}\cdot 2^{\lvert\mathord{\mathcal{P}}\rvert}}\cdot 2^{C_{8}\cdot\lvert\mathord{\mathcal{P}}\rvert\cdot 2^{\lvert\mathord{\mathcal{P}}\rvert}})

\displaystyle=\;
